# FIX AutoTest 测试用例详解

> YAML 格式用例位于 `FixAutoTest/FixAutoTest/testcase/*.yaml`

## Excel → YAML 对应关系

| Excel (.xlsx) | YAML (.yaml) | Steps | 多回报 | INI |
|--------------|-------------|:---:|:---:|------|
| orderinsert-00001-1.xlsx | orderinsert-00001-1.yaml | 7 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| orderinsert-00001-3.xlsx | orderinsert-00001-3.yaml | 26 | 15 | fix42-01 |
| orderinsert-00002-2.xlsx | orderinsert-00002-2.yaml | 6 | 6 | fix42-02 |
| MassQuote.xlsx | MassQuote.yaml | 9 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| TJOrder-00003.xlsx | TJOrder-00003.yaml | 30 | 15 | fix42-03 |
| Position-00010-1.xlsx | Position-00010-1.yaml | 14 | 0 | fix42-10 |
| PositionMaintenance-00010-3.xlsx | PositionMaintenance-00010-3.yaml | 16 | 8 | fix42-10 |
| orderCancelReplace-00010-4.xlsx | orderCancelReplace-00010-4.yaml | 42 | 36 | fix42-10 |
| TAS-00001-4.xlsx | TAS-00001-4.yaml | 7 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| TAS-00001-6.xlsx | TAS-00001-6.yaml | 11 | 10 | fix42-01 |
| TAS-00002-5.xlsx | TAS-00002-5.yaml | 6 | 6 | fix42-02 |
| Hy&BDQry-00010-2.xlsx | Hy&BDQry-00010-2.yaml | 4 | 2 | fix42-10 |

## YAML 格式说明

```yaml
name: "orderinsert-00001-1"
steps:
  - step: 1
    request:
      MsgType: D           # 可读标签名，加载时转为数字 Tag
      Side: Buy            # 可读值名，Buy → 1
      OrdType: Limit       # Limit → 2
      Account: "00001"     # 引号保留前导零
    expect:                # 单回报
      MsgType: 8
      OrdStatus: Rejected
      match: [MsgType, OrdStatus, ClOrdID]  # 比对字段
  - step: 2
    request:
      MsgType: D
      ...
    expects:               # 多回报
      - MsgType: 8
        ClOrdID: "2"
        match: [...]
      - MsgType: 8         # 第二笔回报
        ClOrdID: "2"
        match: [...]
```

> **注意:** `caselist.txt` 已改为引用 `.yaml` 文件。Excel 原文件保留在 `testcase/` 目录供参考。
